1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The structure variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 371 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 975 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 371 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the structure structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 975. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
